{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory — recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。Metrics 查 registry。\\n\\n## 决策原因\\n\\n- **CORAL 需要充分训练**: epochs=5 只得 53.7%，100 epochs 后 78.1%。d1v2 AUC 从 0.34→0.82。之前\\\"CORAL 不适合此任务\\\"的结论是训练不足导致的误判。(2026-05-14)\\n- **CE-MLP 可能也训练不足**: baseline battery 首次跑 CE-MLP 用了 5 epochs 得 74.9%，需要用默认参数重跑确认。(2026-05-14)\\n- **解码策略影响是模型依赖的 (D006, updated)**: Pythia temp09 降 1.9pp，但 GPT-2 XL temp09 升 2.1pp，OLMo temp09 升 3.26pp。两个模型 temp09 优于 nucleus，一个相反。论文不能说\\\"strategy-agnostic\\\"，应说\\\"strategy-dependent magnitude with consistent signal presence\\\"。(2026-05-15)\\n- **OLMo temp09 是当前最强配置 (D008)**: 82.03% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.901。OLMo + temperature sampling 的组合产生最强深度信号。可能原因：OLMo 的 Dolma 训练数据分布 + temperature 的平滑概率重分配互相增强。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Feature importance 表述 (D007)**: top feature set (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) 跨 cells 稳定，但 ranking 因模型而异。Claim 2 应说\\\"consistent feature set\\\"而非\\\"var_surprisal as single best\\\"。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Top-k 信号衰减**: Pythia top-k 69.6% vs nucleus 78.2% (-8.6pp)，截断式采样比连续概率重分配保留更弱的深度信号。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Atlas v2 EDD 差异 (D009)**: 6/7 cells EDD=3（信号持续无饱和），但 Pythia topk50 EDD=1（d2v3 AUC=0.692，唯一<0.80）。\\\"小模型+受限采样→退化信号更早饱和\\\"是 paper Discussion 的核心论点。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Atlas v2 Feature trajectory (D010)**: p90_ppl/var_surprisal/mean_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减（ρ=-1.0），rep_2gram/3gram/4gram 随 depth 单调递增。递归生成同时降低 surprise + 增加重复，两类信号互补。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD 推荐阈值 T=0.75 (D011, 2026-05-15)**: 9-cell sensitivity 分析显示 T=0.65→0.80 对应 EDD=3 cell 数 9/9→5/9。T=0.75 下 7/9 维持 EDD=3，仅 topk 掉队。瓶颈统一在 d2v3 pair，d0v1(>0.98)从不是限制因素。\\n- **topk 跨模型一致性最高 (D012, 2026-05-15)**: topk50 策略跨模型 ρ=0.811，高于 nucleus 和 temp09。确定性截断使不同模型的 feature landscape 趋同。但这同时伴随信号衰减（所有模型 topk 的 acc 最低）。\\n\\n## DCOL 系统性失败分析 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**5 次连续失败，覆盖 full fine-tuning + LoRA 两种策略：**\\n- v1: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-3 → embedding collapse\\n- v2: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-4 → OOM zombie\\n- v3: Full FT → Director 决策转 LoRA, killed\\n- v4: LoRA r=16, margin_lr=1e-3 → 违反硬约束 killed\\n- v5: LoRA r=16 alpha=16, margin_lr=1e-4 → NaN loss 3000+ steps\\n\\n**根因判断**: 非调参问题。v5 在正确超参下仍 NaN，指向 DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 的根本性数值不稳定。若重试应换模型（如 RoBERTa-large）。\\n\\n**Framing 影响**: DCOL Contingency 已激活。Atlas/EDD 为 primary contribution，DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section。已向 Director 提交 3 方案 Framing 调整提案（A=激活contingency / B=纯empirical / C=换模型重试），等待决策。\\n\\n## 文献调研：解码策略对文本统计特征的影响 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**核心发现：我们\\\"解码策略效应是模型依赖的\\\"这一 finding 新颖性等级为 partially-known。** 解码策略效应的模型/任务依赖性已被文献确认，但将其应用于递归生成深度信号检测是新的贡献。\\n\\n关键论文：\\n1. **Garces Arias et al. (COLING 2025)** \\\"Decoding Decoded: Understanding Hyperparameter Effects in Open-Ended Text Generation\\\" — 最直接相关。大规模实证研究证实最优解码配置因模型而异，GPT2-XL 选对策略可胜过 Falcon2-11B。**支持**我们的模型依赖发现，但他们测的是文本质量指标，非递归深度信号。\\n2. **Wiher, Meister & Cotterell (TACL 2022)** \\\"On Decoding Strategies for Neural Text Generators\\\" — 证明解码策略效应不跨任务迁移（task-dependent），我们的发现将此扩展到 model-dependent 维度。**补充**关系。\\n3. **Holtzman et al. (ICLR 2020)** \\\"The Curious Case of Neural Text Degeneration\\\" — 奠基性工作，证明 nucleus sampling 统计分布最接近人类文本，top-k 多样性不足。**支持**我们 top-k 跨模型一致最弱的观察。\\n4. **Shumailov et al. (Nature 2024)** \\\"AI models collapse when trained on recursively generated data\\\" — 递归生成导致模型坍塌，低 temperature (0.3) 坍塌更快。与我们的递归生成设置直接相关，但他们关注训练坍塌而非深度信号检测。**补充**关系。\\n5. **Basu et al. (ICLR 2021)** \\\"Mirostat\\\" — 证明 perplexity 与 p 近线性但与 k 高度非线性，可解释 top-k 产生更弱深度信号的机制。**补充**关系。\\n\\n**文献空白**：无论文研究解码策略如何影响递归生成深度的*可检测性*，也无论文报告过我们观察到的具体模式（temp-only 对某些模型增强信号但对另一些削弱）。这是我们的独特贡献。\\n\\n## 约束\\n\\n- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用\\n- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`\\n- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）\\n- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）\\n- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）\\n- OpenAI SDK base_url: 代码无需修改，v1.0+ 自动读取 OPENAI_BASE_URL 环境变量\\n- DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容（DCOL v5 NaN 证实）\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n- kill 实验时必须 `ps -p PID` + `nvidia-smi` 验证进程确实死亡\\n- 僵尸进程会导致后续实验 OOM\\n- conda 环境不要混用 pip install，统一用一种\\n- sync_code 前确保本地 commit 已 push\\n- Baseline epochs 参数很重要——默认值通常比手动设的小值好得多\\n- Worker 池耗尽时 request_secret 不可挂载给 Worker，需主 Agent 层面获取后传递\\n- DCOL 类 contrastive ordinal loss 对 backbone 数值稳定性要求极高——DeBERTa 不适合，应考虑 RoBERTa\\n\", \"total_chars\": 4074, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

Excellent findings. Key result: 1 sample (sample_id=6923) with mean_ppl=1.43e13 contributes 94.4% of depth-1 mean_ppl total. p90_ppl is perfectly monotonic (566.8 → 143.0 → 91.7 → 73.1, ρ=-0.853). This is a positive finding for the paper — it explains why percentile features are more robust than mean.

I should:
1. Record this in memory.md (non-metrics insight)
2. Update atlas_v2_analysis notes


3. Report to Director

I also need to add the data source authority note to memory.md as planned from the previous Director instruction.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory — recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。Metrics 查 registry。\\n\\n## 决策原因\\n\\n- **CORAL 需要充分训练**: epochs=5 只得 53.7%，100 epochs 后 78.1%。d1v2 AUC 从 0.34→0.82。之前\\\"CORAL 不适合此任务\\\"的结论是训练不足导致的误判。(2026-05-14)\\n- **CE-MLP 可能也训练不足**: baseline battery 首次跑 CE-MLP 用了 5 epochs 得 74.9%，需要用默认参数重跑确认。(2026-05-14)\\n- **解码策略影响是模型依赖的 (D006, updated)**: Pythia temp09 降 1.9pp，但 GPT-2 XL temp09 升 2.1pp，OLMo temp09 升 3.26pp。两个模型 temp09 优于 nucleus，一个相反。论文不能说\\\"strategy-agnostic\\\"，应说\\\"strategy-dependent magnitude with consistent signal presence\\\"。(2026-05-15)\\n- **OLMo temp09 是当前最强配置 (D008)**: 82.03% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.901。OLMo + temperature sampling 的组合产生最强深度信号。可能原因：OLMo 的 Dolma 训练数据分布 + temperature 的平滑概率重分配互相增强。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Feature importance 表述 (D007)**: top feature set (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) 跨 cells 稳定，但 ranking 因模型而异。Claim 2 应说\\\"consistent feature set\\\"而非\\\"var_surprisal as single best\\\"。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Top-k 信号衰减**: Pythia top-k 69.6% vs nucleus 78.2% (-8.6pp)，截断式采样比连续概率重分配保留更弱的深度信号。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Atlas v2 EDD 差异 (D009)**: 6/7 cells EDD=3（信号持续无饱和），但 Pythia topk50 EDD=1（d2v3 AUC=0.692，唯一<0.80）。\\\"小模型+受限采样→退化信号更早饱和\\\"是 paper Discussion 的核心论点。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Atlas v2 Feature trajectory (D010)**: p90_ppl/var_surprisal/mean_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减（ρ=-1.0），rep_2gram/3gram/4gram 随 depth 单调递增。递归生成同时降低 surprise + 增加重复，两类信号互补。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD 推荐阈值 T=0.75 (D011, 2026-05-15)**: 9-cell sensitivity 分析显示 T=0.65→0.80 对应 EDD=3 cell 数 9/9→5/9。T=0.75 下 7/9 维持 EDD=3，仅 topk 掉队。瓶颈统一在 d2v3 pair，d0v1(>0.98)从不是限制因素。\\n- **topk 跨模型一致性最高 (D012, 2026-05-15)**: topk50 策略跨模型 ρ=0.811，高于 nucleus 和 temp09。确定性截断使不同模型的 feature landscape 趋同。但这同时伴随信号衰减（所有模型 topk 的 acc 最低）。\\n\\n## DCOL 系统性失败分析 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**5 次连续失败，覆盖 full fine-tuning + LoRA 两种策略：**\\n- v1: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-3 → embedding collapse\\n- v2: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-4 → OOM zombie\\n- v3: Full FT → Director 决策转 LoRA, killed\\n- v4: LoRA r=16, margin_lr=1e-3 → 违反硬约束 killed\\n- v5: LoRA r=16 alpha=16, margin_lr=1e-4 → NaN loss 3000+ steps\\n\\n**根因判断**: 非调参问题。v5 在正确超参下仍 NaN，指向 DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 的根本性数值不稳定。若重试应换模型（如 RoBERTa-large）。\\n\\n**Framing 影响**: DCOL Contingency 已激活。Atlas/EDD 为 primary contribution，DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section。已向 Director 提交 3 方案 Framing 调整提案（A=激活contingency / B=纯empirical / C=换模型重试），等待决策。\\n\\n## 文献调研：解码策略对文本统计特征的影响 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**核心发现：我们\\\"解码策略效应是模型依赖的\\\"这一 finding 新颖性等级为 partially-known。** 解码策略效应的模型/任务依赖性已被文献确认，但将其应用于递归生成深度信号检测是新的贡献。\\n\\n关键论文：\\n1. **Garces Arias et al. (COLING 2025)** \\\"Decoding Decoded: Understanding Hyperparameter Effects in Open-Ended Text Generation\\\" — 最直接相关。大规模实证研究证实最优解码配置因模型而异，GPT2-XL 选对策略可胜过 Falcon2-11B。**支持**我们的模型依赖发现，但他们测的是文本质量指标，非递归深度信号。\\n2. **Wiher, Meister & Cotterell (TACL 2022)** \\\"On Decoding Strategies for Neural Text Generators\\\" — 证明解码策略效应不跨任务迁移（task-dependent），我们的发现将此扩展到 model-dependent 维度。**补充**关系。\\n3. **Holtzman et al. (ICLR 2020)** \\\"The Curious Case of Neural Text Degeneration\\\" — 奠基性工作，证明 nucleus sampling 统计分布最接近人类文本，top-k 多样性不足。**支持**我们 top-k 跨模型一致最弱的观察。\\n4. **Shumailov et al. (Nature 2024)** \\\"AI models collapse when trained on recursively generated data\\\" — 递归生成导致模型坍塌，低 temperature (0.3) 坍塌更快。与我们的递归生成设置直接相关，但他们关注训练坍塌而非深度信号检测。**补充**关系。\\n5. **Basu et al. (ICLR 2021)** \\\"Mirostat\\\" — 证明 perplexity 与 p 近线性但与 k 高度非线性，可解释 top-k 产生更弱深度信号的机制。**补充**关系。\\n\\n**文献空白**：无论文研究解码策略如何影响递归生成深度的*可检测性*，也无论文报告过我们观察到的具体模式（temp-only 对某些模型增强信号但对另一些削弱）。这是我们的独特贡献。\\n\\n## 约束\\n\\n- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用\\n- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`\\n- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）\\n- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）\\n- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）\\n- OpenAI SDK base_url: 代码无需修改，v1.0+ 自动读取 OPENAI_BASE_URL 环境变量\\n- DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容（DCOL v5 NaN 证实）\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n- kill 实验时必须 `ps -p PID` + `nvidia-smi` 验证进程确实死亡\\n- 僵尸进程会导致后续实验 OOM\\n- conda 环境不要混用 pip install，统一用一种\\n- sync_code 前确保本地 commit 已 push\\n- Baseline epochs 参数很重要——默认值通常比手动设的小值好得多\\n- Worker 池耗尽时 request_secret 不可挂载给 Worker，需主 Agent 层面获取后传递\\n- DCOL 类 contrastive ordinal loss 对 backbone 数值稳定性要求极高——DeBERTa 不适合，应考虑 RoBERTa\\n\", \"total_chars\": 4074, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

趋同。但这同时伴随信号衰减（所有模型 topk 的 acc 最低）。\\n\\n## DCOL 系统性失败分析 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**5 次连续失败，覆盖 full fine-tuning + LoRA 两种策略：**\\n- v1: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-3 → embedding collapse\\n- v2: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-4 → OOM zombie\\n- v3: Full FT → Director 决策转 LoRA, killed\\n- v4: LoRA r=16, margin_lr=1e-3 → 违反硬约束 killed\\n- v5: LoRA r=16 alpha=16, margin_lr=1e-4 → NaN loss 3000+ contribution，DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section。已向 Director 提交 3 方案 Framing 调整提案（A=激活contingency / B=纯empirical / C=换模型重试），等待决策。\\n\\n## 文献调研：解码策略对文本统计特征的影响 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**核心发现：我们\\\"解码策略效应是模型依赖的\\\"这一 finding 新颖性等级为 partially-known。** 解码策略效应的模型/任务依赖性已被文献确认，但将其应用于递归生成深度信号检测是新的贡献。\\n\\n关键论文：\\n1. **Garces Arias et al. Discussion 的核心论点。(2026-05-15)\n- **Atlas v2 Feature trajectory (D010)**: p90_ppl/var_surprisal/mean_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减（ρ=-1.0），rep_2gram/3gram/4gram 随 depth 单调递增。递归生成同时降低 surprise + 增加重复，两类信号互补。(2026-05-15)\n- **EDD 推荐阈值 T=0.75 (D011, 2026-05-15)**: 9-cell sensitivity 分析显示 T=0.65→0.80 对应 EDD=3 cell 数 9/9→5/9。T=0.75 下 7/9 维持 EDD=3，仅 topk 掉队。瓶颈统一在 d2v3 pair，d0v1(>0.98)从不是限制因素。\n- **topk 跨模型一致性最高 (D012, 2026-05-15)**: topk50 策略跨模型 ρ=0.811，高于 nucleus 和 temp09。确定性截断使不同模型的 feature landscape 趋同。但这同时伴随信号衰减（所有模型 topk 的 acc 最低）。\n- **p90_ppl 优于 mean_ppl 的统计根因 (2026-05-15)**: olmo_topk50 depth_1 中 1 个样本 (mean_ppl=1.43e13) 贡献了总 mean_ppl 的 94.4%。Token-level perplexity 极度重尾（kurtosis>4900, CV=67×）。即使剔除极端值，mean_ppl 仍非单调。p90_ppl 完美单调（ρ=-0.853, CV=0.28-0.67），论文应说明 percentile-based features 的统计稳健性优势。\n- **Cross-strategy LOO temp09 d1v2 反转 (D014, 2026-05-15)**: 非 bug，是真实现象。temp09 的 d1→d2 退化轨迹与 nucleus/topk50 根本不同，RF 在其他策略上学到的判别规则在 temp09 上方向反转。纳入 Claim 2 \"decoding strategy shapes degradation trajectory\"。\n\n## DCOL 系统性失败分析 (2026-05-15)\n\n**5 次连续失败，覆盖 full fine-tuning + LoRA 两种策略：**\n- v1: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-3 → embedding collapse\n- v2: Full FT, margin_lr=1e-4 → OOM zombie\n- v3: Full FT → Director 决策转 LoRA, killed\n- v4: LoRA r=16, margin_lr=1e-3 → 违反硬约束 killed\n- v5: LoRA r=16 alpha=16, margin_lr=1e-4 → NaN loss 3000+ steps\n\n**根因判断**: 非调参问题。v5 在正确超参下仍 NaN，指向 DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 的根本性数值不稳定。v6 换用 RoBERTa-large。\n\n**Framing 影响**: DCOL Contingency 已激活 (D011)。Atlas/EDD 为 primary contribution，DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section。\n\n## 文献调研：解码策略对文本统计特征的影响 (2026-05-15)\n\n**核心发现：我们\"解码策略效应是模型依赖的\"这一 finding 新颖性等级为 partially-known。** 解码策略效应的模型/任务依赖性已被文献确认，但将其应用于递归生成深度信号检测是新的贡献。\n\n关键论文：\n1. **Garces Arias et al. (COLING 2025)** \"Decoding Decoded\" — 最直接相关，证实最优解码配置因模型而异。\n2. **Wiher, Meister & Cotterell (TACL 2022)** — 证明解码策略效应 task-dependent。\n3. **Holtzman et al. (ICLR 2020)** \"The Curious Case of Neural Text Degeneration\" — nucleus sampling 奠基。\n4. **Shumailov et al. (Nature 2024)** — 递归生成导致模型坍塌。\n5. **Basu et al. (ICLR 2021)** \"Mirostat\" — perplexity 与采样参数的非线性关系。\n\n## 数据源权威性 (2026-05-15)\n\n- **AUC 数据**: pipeline `pairwise_auc.json` 为 ground truth，`edd_sensitivity.json` 同步后作为 EDD 分析引用源\n- **EDD 统计**: `edd_sensitivity.json` 为权威数据源，论文不引用 summary markdown\n- **RF accuracy**: 各实验 pipeline 结果为 ground truth，`baseline_battery_9cell_001` registry 为汇总引用源\n- **Bootstrap AUC (edd_analysis_9cell.json)**: 与 pipeline 偏差大（如 gpt2xl_topk50 d1v2 差 8.8pp），论文不引用\n\n## 约束\n\n- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用\n- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`\n- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）\n- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）\n- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）\n- OpenAI SDK base_url: 代码无需修改，v1.0+ 自动读取 OPENAI_BASE_URL 环境变量\n- DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容（DCOL v5 NaN 证实）\n\n## 经验教训\n\n- kill 实验时必须 `ps -p PID` + `nvidia-smi` 验证进程确实死亡\n- 僵尸进程会导致后续实验 OOM\n- conda 环境不要混用 pip install，统一用一种\n- sync_code 前确保本地 commit 已 push\n- Baseline epochs 参数很重要——默认值通常比手动设的小值好得多\n- Worker 池耗尽时 request_secret 不可挂载给 Worker，需主 Agent 层面获取后传递\n- DCOL 类 contrastive ordinal loss 对 backbone 数值稳定性要求极高——DeBERTa 不适合，应考虑 RoBERTa\n- submit_training_job 可能不自动转 RUNNING（需手动 update_registry 推进状态并补充 provenance spec）\n"

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user is the Director of an ML research project "recursive_gen_depth_est" (recursive generation depth estimation). The session involved executing a Plan A Sprint for EMNLP ARR May 25 submission (10-day deadline). Key intents:
   - Fix registry inconsistencies (dcol_deberta_004/005)
   - Restart batch_feature_extraction on cuda:0
   - Execute plan_011 (depth extension d4/d5 for Pythia-1.4B nucleus)
   - Run parallel CPU experiments: plan_005 (cross-model LOO), plan_007 (cross-strategy LOO), Atlas figures (4 paper figures), 9-cell baseline battery
   - Prepare and submit DCOL v6 with RoBERTa-large (replacing DeBERTa) on cuda:1
   - Fix multiple figure bugs (EDD off-by-one, strategy labels, confusion matrix consistency, accuracy verification)
   - Unify 3 conflicting AUC data sources (pipeline pairwise_auc.json as ground truth)
   - Investigate olmo_topk50 depth_1 mean_ppl=3e9 outlier
   - Register Atlas v2 formal analysis and 9-cell baseline battery in registry
   - Fix EDD summary statistics (T=0.75: 2.67→2.78, T=0.80: 2.56→2.44)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Recursive generation depth estimation: predicting how many model→data→model recursion cycles text has undergone
   - 3×3 experiment matrix: 3 models (Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B, GPT-2 XL) × 3 decoding strategies (nucleus p=0.95, temperature τ=0.9, top-k k=50)
   - Atlas/EDD (Effective Discrimination Depth): primary contribution measuring how deep recursive signals persist
   - EDD calculation: check adjacent-depth AUC pairs (d0v1, d1v2, d2v3), EDD = idx+1 where idx is first pair below threshold T=0.75
   - DCOL (Contrastive Ordinal Loss): secondary contribution, 5 failures with DeBERTa, v6 uses RoBERTa-large
   - LoRA fine-tuning: r=16, alpha=32 (Pythia), r=16 alpha=16 (DCOL)
   - Feature importance: p90_ppl and var_surprisal consistently top-2 across 9/9 and 7/9 cells respectively
   - Cross-model LOO: train on 2 model families, test on 3rd (mean acc 0.664)
   - Cross-strategy LOO: per-model, train on 2 strategies, test on 3rd (mean acc 0.591)
   - D014 finding: temp09 d1v2 AUC inversion when held out (GPT-2 XL: 0.13, OLMo: 0.25) — real phenomenon, not bug
   - p90_ppl vs mean_ppl: mean_ppl fails due to extreme heavy-tailed token perplexity (1 sample = 94.4% of depth-1 total)
   - Registry state machine: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → DONE
   - Worker spawn model: Main Agent coordinates, Workers execute SSH/code/analysis tasks
   - submit_training_job uses tmux under /bin/sh, requires bash -c wrapper for source/conda

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `src/plot_atlas_figures.py` (server: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/)
     - Central figure generation script for 4 paper figures
     - Bug 1 fix: Line ~98 `edd_val = idx + 2` → `edd_val = idx + 1` (went through idx as intermediate wrong fix)
     - Bug 2 fix: Figure 2 panel titles now include strategy labels ("Pythia-1.4B (temp τ=0.9)", etc.)
     - Bug 3 fix: Figure 3 titles use CV accuracy instead of hardcoded values
     - Bug 4 fix: Verified OLMo nucleus RF=0.788 is correct
     - Pending refactor: Load AUC data from JSON files instead of hardcoding (unify_auc Worker)
   
   - `src/dcol_trainer_v6.py` (server)
     - New DCOL trainer using RoBERTa-large instead of DeBERTa
     - LoRA targets: ["query", "value"] (RoBERTa-specific)
     - Added: bf16 autocast, gradient clipping max_norm=1.0, NaN early stop, collapse guard (step 500 acc<30% kill)
     - Dry-run passed 5 steps, 357M total params / 1.89M trainable (0.53%)
     - Model path: /root/autodl-tmp/models/roberta-large-ms/AI-ModelScope/roberta-large
   
   - `scripts/run_depth_extension.sh` (server)
     - 7-step pipeline: finetune d3 → generate d4 → finetune d4 → generate d5 → feature extraction (6-class) → RF classification → data summary
     - Same config as parent mvp_pipeline_pythia_002
   
   - `scripts/extract_features.py` (server)
     - Modified to add --max_depth parameter (default 3, backward compatible)
   
   - `src/cross_model_loo.py` (server) — Cross-model LOO analysis script
   - `src/cross_strategy_loo.py` (server) — Cross-strategy LOO analysis script
   - `src/atlas_v2_analysis.py` (server) — Formal 9-cell Atlas analysis
   - `src/run_9cell_battery.py` (server) — 9-cell baseline battery (RF/LR/CORAL/DivEye)
   
   - `memory.md` (project file)
     - Updated with: p90_ppl vs mean_ppl root cause, D014 temp09 inversion, data source authority hierarchy, submit_training_job registry lesson
   
   - `artifacts/figures/fig1_auc_decay.{pdf,png}` — AUC decay curve with EDD threshold
   - `artifacts/figures/fig2_feature_trajectory.{pdf,png}` — Feature trajectory panel
   - `artifacts/figures/fig3_confusion_matrix.{pdf,png}` — Best vs worst confusion matrix
   - `artifacts/figures/fig4_atlas_heatmap.{pdf,png}` — 3×3 Atlas heatmap
   - `artifacts/atlas_v2/` — 4 JSON files: feature_importance_9cells, monotonicity_scores_9cells, edd_analysis_9cells, cross_comparison_9cells
   - `artifacts/olmo_topk50_mean_ppl_outlier_analysis.md` — Outlier analysis with paper-ready text

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Registry state machine violation (PREPARING→DONE)**:
     - cross_strategy_loo_001 couldn't go directly from PREPARING to DONE
     - Fixed by stepping through: QUEUED → RUNNING → DONE
   
   - **submit_on_allocated failed ("missing server/gpu in registry")**:
     - dcol_roberta_v6_001 had server/gpu in spec but not at top level
     - Fixed by using submit_training_job instead which handles this
   
   - **submit_training_job bash syntax failure**:
     - Command used `source` which is bash-only, but tmux runs under /bin/sh
     - Error: "EXIT_CODE=127 in 1-3 min"
     - Fixed by wrapping in `bash -c "..."`
   
   - **EDD formula triple-fix**:
     - Original: `edd_val = idx + 2` (too high, EDD=4 for topk cells)
     - First fix: `edd_val = idx` (over-corrected, EDD=2)
     - Director corrected: `edd_val = idx + 1` (correct, EDD=3 for topk cells)
     - Lesson: EDD = number of passed pairs = idx + 1 where idx is failure position
   
   - **Registry ALLOCATED→RUNNING transition**:
     - submit_training_job wrote PID but didn't auto-transition status
     - Manual update_registry(status=RUNNING) failed first time: "Eval experiment requires checkpoint_source, eval_data"
     - Fixed by adding checkpoint_source="N/A" and eval_data="N/A" to spec
   
   - **read_registry filter by exp_id returns empty**:
     - Filters don't support exp_id lookup directly
     - Workaround: filter by status or use limit=0 and search in results

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Registry inconsistencies**: Both dcol_deberta_004/005 were already fixed in previous session
   - **Batch feature extraction**: 5 cells all complete (20k lines each), no restart needed

        "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

\"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ |\\n|--------|--------------------------|-----------------|---|\\n| RF acc | 78.15% [76.9, 79.4] | 76.25% [74.9, 77.5] | -1.9pp |\\n| LR acc | 74.92% | 74.05% | -0.9pp |\\n| AUC 0v1 | 0.9975 | 0.9968 | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. \\\"notes\\\": \\\"## pythia_topk50_001 分析结果\\\\n\\\\n### 核心指标\\\\n- RF 4-class acc: 69.6% (chance 25%), PASS\\\\n- RF 3-class acc: 59.2% (chance 33.3%)\\\\n- RF 3-class filtered acc: 61.3%\\\\n- d1v2 AUC: 0.7518, d2v3 AUC: 0.6918\\\\n\\\\n### vs Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) 对比\\\\n| Metric | topk50 | baseline | delta |\\\\n|--------|--------|----------|-------|\\\\n| 4-class acc | 69.6% | 78.2% | -8.6pp |\\\\n| 3-class acc | 59.2% | 70.8% | -11.6pp |\\\\n| d0v1 AUC | 0.9927 | 0.9975 | -0.005 |\\\\n| d1v2 AUC | 0.7518 | 0.8607 | -0.109 |\\\\n| d2v3 AUC | 0.6918 | 0.8005 | -0.109 |\\\\n\\\\n### vs temp09 对比\\\\n| Metric | topk50 | temp09 | delta |\\\\n|--------|--------|--------|-------|\\\\n| 4-class acc | 69.6% | 76.25% | -6.65pp |\\\\n| 3-class acc | 59.2% | 69.83% | -10.6pp |\\\\n| d1v2 AUC | 0.7518 | 0.8327 | -0.081 |\\\\n| d2v3 AUC | 0.6918 | 0.7928 | -0.101 |\\\\n\\\\n### Feature importance 一致性\\\\nTop-5 与 baseline 完全一致: var_surprisal, p90_ppl, mean_ppl, mean_surprisal, p75_ppl\\\\n\\\\n### Code contamination (narrow)\\\\nd0=0.4%, d1=7.7%, d2=12.3%, d3=13.1%（高于 baseline，top-k 产生更多 code-like text）\\\\nFiltered 3-class 性能反而略高于 unfiltered (61.3% vs 59.2%)，说明 code contamination 不是性能的主要驱动\\\\n\\\\n### 结论\\\\nSUCCESS — 深度信号在 top-k 采样下仍然存在（69.6% >> 25% chance），但信号强度显著低于 nucleus (-8.6pp) 和 temp09 (-6.65pp)。\\\\n跨策略泛化成立（信号存在），但不同采样策略对信号强度有影响。Top-k 的截断式采样比连续概率重分配（nucleus/temperature）保留的深度信号更弱。\\\"}, \\\"cross_model_gpt2xl_001\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T14:10:51.209Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\\\n\\\\n### Code contamination\\\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Analysis\\\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. \"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ |\\n|--------|--------------------------|-----------------|---|\\n| RF acc | 78.15% [76.9, 79.4] | 76.25% [74.9, 77.5] | -1.9pp |\\n| LR acc | 74.92% | 74.05% | -0.9pp |\\n| AUC 0v1 | 0.9975 | 0.9968 | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. Hypothesis confirmed: temperature-only decoding preserves recursive depth signal.\"}, \"mvp_pipeline_pythia_002\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T12:07:25.860Z\", \"updated_at\": _source line 700 · 2026-05-15T04:51:30.645Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user (Director) is orchestrating a 10-day sprint toward EMNLP ARR May 25 submission for the `recursive_gen_depth_est` project. The project studies recursive generation depth estimation—predicting how many rounds of model→data→model recursive training cycles text has undergone. Plan A (D011) is activated with Atlas/EDD as primary contribution and DCOL as secondary/negative result. The Director issues sequential instructions to: fix registry inconsistencies, manage GPU utilization (2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB on gpu-6000pro-2), run parallel experiments (cross-model LOO, cross-strategy LOO, depth extension, DCOL v6 with RoBERTa-large), generate paper-quality figures, unify contradictory data sources, and maintain rigorous experiment provenance.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Recursive generation depth estimation: predicting how many finetune→generate cycles text has undergone (4-class: d0/d1/d2/d3)
   - Effective Discrimination Depth (EDD): max depth where adjacent-pair AUC ≥ threshold T
   - Atlas/Depth Degradation Atlas: cross-model × cross-strategy analysis of depth signal degradation
   - DCOL (Contrastive Ordinal Loss): learnable-margin contrastive loss for depth classification
   - LoRA fine-tuning (r=16, alpha=32 for Pythia; r=16, alpha=16 for DCOL)
   - Feature extraction pipeline: 19 statistical features (p90_ppl, var_surprisal, mean_ppl, etc.)
   - RandomForest classifier (n_estimators=200, random_state=42) as primary method
   - Leave-One-Out generalization tests (cross-model and cross-strategy)
   - 3×3 experiment matrix: 3 models (Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B, GPT-2 XL) × 3 strategies (nucleus p=0.95, temp τ=0.9, top-k k=50)
   - Registry state machine: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → DONE
   - Worker-based execution model with spawn_worker, submit_training_job, cron monitoring
   - Constraints C001/C002: must check DCOL failure history before new submissions

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `src/dcol_trainer_v6.py` (server: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/)
     - New file for DCOL v6 with RoBERTa-large replacing DeBERTa
     - Key changes: LoRA target_modules=["query", "value"], bf16 autocast, gradient clipping max_norm=1.0, NaN early stop (NaNLossError), collapse guard (step 500 acc<30% auto-kill), --max_steps parameter
     - Dry-run validated: 357M total params, 1.89M trainable (0.53%), 5 steps no NaN
   
   - `src/plot_atlas_figures.py` (server)
     - Generates 4 paper figures: AUC decay, feature trajectory, confusion matrix, atlas heatmap
     - Bug fixes applied: EDD formula `edd_val = idx + 1` (was idx+2, then overcorrected to idx, then corrected to idx+1)
     - Figure 2: strategy labels added to panel titles (Pythia temp=0.9, OLMo/GPT-2 XL nucleus)
     - Figure 3: title accuracy changed to 5-fold CV computed value
     - Figure 4: all 9 cells confirmed as RF accuracy
     - Pending refactor: Director ordered loading from JSON files instead of hardcoded values (Worker in progress)

   - `scripts/run_depth_extension.sh` (server)
     - New 7-step pipeline: finetune d3 → generate d4 → finetune d4 → generate d5 → feature extraction → RF 6-class → data summary
     - Same config as parent mvp_pipeline_pythia_002
   
   - `src/extract_features.py` (server)
     - Modified: added --max_depth parameter (default 3, backward compatible) for 6-class depth extension
   
   - `src/cross_model_loo.py` (server)
     - Cross-model LOO: train on 2 model families (all strategies), test on 3rd
     - RF(n_estimators=200, random_state=42), 4-class classification
   
   - `src/cross_strategy_loo.py` (server)
     - Cross-strategy LOO: per-model, train on 2 strategies, test on 3rd
   
   - `src/atlas_v2_analysis.py` (server)
     - 9-cell Atlas v2 formal analysis: feature importance, monotonicity, EDD, cross-model comparison
   
   - `memory.md` (project knowledge file)
     - Updated with: D014 temp09 d1v2 reversal finding, DCOL v6 status, data source authority hierarchy
     - Data source authority: pipeline pairwise_auc.json for AUC, edd_sensitivity.json for EDD stats, pipeline classification JSON for RF accuracy, atlas_v2 feature_importance_9cells.json for features
   
   - `artifacts/figures/fig1_auc_decay.{pdf,png}` — AUC decay curves, EDD threshold T=0.75, 7/9 EDD>3
   - `artifacts/figures/fig2_feature_trajectory.{pdf,png}` — 3 models × 5 features trajectory
   - `artifacts/figures/fig3_confusion_matrix.{pdf,png}` — best vs worst cell comparison
   - `artifacts/figures/fig4_atlas_heatmap.{pdf,png}` — 3×3 accuracy heatmap
   - `artifacts/atlas_v2/` — 4 JSON files from formal analysis
   - `results/baseline_battery_9cell/summary_9cell.json` — 9-cell baseline results
   - `results/cross_model_loo.json` — cross-model LOO results
   - `results/cross_strategy_loo.json` — cross-strategy LOO results

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Registry state transition error**: PREPARING → DONE not allowed. Fix: must go PREPARING → QUEUED → RUNNING → DONE sequentially.
   
   - **EDD off-by-one (3 iterations)**:
     - Original: `edd_val = idx + 2` (wrong, EDD too high)
     - First fix: `edd_val = idx` (overcorrection, EDD too low, Pythia topk showed EDD=2)
     - Director feedback: "你改成了 idx，导致 EDD=2，这是过度修正"
     - Final fix: `edd_val = idx + 1` (correct, Pythia topk=3, OLMo topk=3, matches atlas_v1_analysis)
   
   - **submit_on_allocated failed**: "Experiment missing server/gpu in registry". Fix: used submit_training_job instead.
   
   - **submit_training_job bash syntax error**: "command uses bash-only syntax (source) but isn't wrapped in bash -c". tmux runs under /bin/sh. Fix: wrapped entire command in `bash -c "..."`.
   
   - **Registry stuck at ALLOCATED after submit_training_job**: submit_training_job wrote PID but didn't auto-transition to RUNNING. Fix: manual update_registry with required fields (needed checkpoint_source and eval_data for experiments with parent).
   
   - **Spawn Worker collision**: atlas_v2_formal Worker already active when tried to spawn again. The system had auto-spawned it from earlier context.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Registry inconsistency**: dcol_deberta_004 and 005 were already fixed in prior session, confirmed and reported.
   - **Batch feature extraction**: Investigated prior data contamination fix, found all 5 cells completed (20k lines each), updated registry.
   - **DCOL model switch**: Cancelled DeBERTa v6 prep Worker, spawned new RoBERTa-large prep Worker per D011 decision.
   - **Pythia nucleus features missing**: For Figure 2, Pythia has no nucleus features file, adopted backup plan with strategy labels in panel titles.
   - **Cross-strategy temp09 d1v2 reversal**: Verified not a label bug via dedicated verification Worker (in-dist AUC >0.95, label mapping consistent). Confirmed as real cross-strategy transfer phenomenon.
   - **Multiple AUC data sources**: Identified 3 contradictory sources (pipeline, edd_sensitivity, edd_analysis_9cell). Director decided pipeline pairwise_auc.json = ground truth. Unification Worker in progress.
   - **EDD summary stats errors**: T=0.75 mean 2.67→2.78, T=0.80 mean 2.56→2.44. Fixed.

6. All User Messages:
   - "[Director 指令] Reviewer 发现两个问题... Registry 不一致 + 新约束 C002"
   - "Continue from where you left off." (multiple times)
   - "[Director 指令] 两张卡空着不行。立即派 Worker 重启 cuda:0 的 batch_feature_extraction"
   - "[WORKER DONE] worker_ssh_batch_feature_extraction_restart" 
   - "[Director 指令] cuda:0 → (A) plan_011 Depth extension to 5 generations"

        "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",
        "numLines": 50,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 50
      }

p75_ppl\\\\n\\\\n### Code contamination (narrow)\\\\nd0=0.4%, d1=7.7%, d2=12.3%, d3=13.1%（高于 baseline，top-k 产生更多 code-like text）\\\\nFiltered 3-class 性能反而略高于 unfiltered (61.3% vs 59.2%)，说明 code contamination 不是性能的主要驱动\\\\n\\\\n### 结论\\\\nSUCCESS — 深度信号在 top-k 采样下仍然存在（69.6% >> 25% chance），但信号强度显著低于 nucleus (-8.6pp) 和 temp09 (-6.65pp)。\\\\n跨策略泛化成立（信号存在），但不同采样策略对信号强度有影响。Top-k 的截断式采样比连续概率重分配（nucleus/temperature）保留的深度信号更弱。\\\"}, \\\"cross_model_gpt2xl_001\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T14:10:51.209Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": +2.7pp |\\\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Analysis\\\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. chance），但信号强度显著低于 nucleus (-8.6pp) 和 temp09 (-6.65pp)。\\n跨策略泛化成立（信号存在），但不同采样策略对信号强度有影响。Top-k 的截断式采样比连续概率重分配（nucleus/temperature）保留的深度信号更弱。\"}, \"cross_model_gpt2xl_001\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of ### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
